**[4:24:44] Sarah Powers:** Yeah, it's it's a great question. I mean, the primary So, I think a couple things. We do that for a couple of reasons. first is we want to understand how the community feels about the project. It gives us an opportunity to interact directly with the land owners. Those downstream impacts are called induced economic impacts. So the total economic footprint of this economic activity or any economic activity is bigger than just those direct expenditures.
